TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，将 block-encoding、qubitization、QSP、QSVT 与 GQSP 统一为一个多项式变换框架，提出端到端算法设计流水线与决策工作流，并应用于典型问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代量子算法日益被表述为对算子和奇异值实施多项式变换的相干过程，但相关工具（block-encoding、qubitization、QSP、QSVT、GQSP）分散且术语各异，缺乏统一视角与选型准则，导致设计者难以判断何时使用 GQSP、qubitization、Laurent 展开或 QSVT。

Method: 将五类核心工具从第一性原理构建为一个统一框架：block-encoding 把非酉矩阵嵌入更大的酉算子；qubitization 将 block-encoding 转化为结构化算子；QSP、QSVT、GQSP 以近最优查询复杂度实现多项式变换。并给出系统化决策工作流，按算子结构与目标变换多项式选择合适方法，同时把算法设计组织为端到端流水线：识别目标矩阵函数、构造 block-encoding、确定谱域、设计多项式或 Laurent 多项式逼近、合成相位因子、翻译为可执行量子线路。

Result: 建立了一个统一的量子算法构造框架与系统化决策流程，明确了直接使用 GQSP、经由 qubitization、Laurent 展开或 QSVT 的适用条件；并通过代表性应用展示了不同算法可由统一的算子变换序列构造，验证了该框架在设计原则与实现方法上的实用性。

Conclusion: 基于多项式变换的统一视角为量子算法设计提供了强大的通用语言与实用方法论，能指导推理、设计与实现量子算法，并为不同问题间的算法构造建立可复用的结构化流程。

Abstract: Modern quantum algorithms are increasingly formulated as coherent procedures for implementing polynomial transformations of operators and singular values. This perspective provides a powerful and unifying language for quantum algorithm design, connecting a wide range of distinct problems through five closely related key tools: block-encoding, qubitization, QSP, QSVT and GQSP. Block-encoding embeds non-unitary matrices into larger unitaries; qubitization converts block-encodings into structured operators; QSP, QSVT and GQSP enable polynomial transformations with near-optimal query complexity. Together, these techniques form a general toolkit for transforming matrix functions into implementable quantum circuits. This paper develops these techniques from first principles as a unified framework for constructing quantum algorithms. We apply this framework to representative applications to highlight design principles and demonstrate how distinct algorithms can be constructed from a unified sequence of operator transformations.
  A central contribution is a systematic decision workflow for selecting the appropriate approach according to the operator structure and the desired transformation polynomial. This perspective clarifies when direct GQSP or through qubitization, or Laurent expansion, or QSVT is most appropriate. We organize algorithmic design into an end-to-end pipeline: identifying the target matrix function, constructing an appropriate block-encoding, determining the relevant spectral domain, designing a polynomial or Laurent-polynomial approximation, synthesizing the phase factors, and translating the transformation into an executable quantum circuit. By applying this unified framework to example applications, we showcase a practical methodology for reasoning, designing, and implementing quantum algorithms based on polynomial transformations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPCQC平台包含多个在规模、拓扑、原生门和噪声特性上各异的QPU。噪声设备中误差沿编译后电路迅速累积，最大化执行保真度对可靠结果至关重要。保真度取决于针对特定目标设备的编译，同一高层电路在不同QPU上会产生不同可执行文件与不同期望保真度。穷举式保真度分配需将每个电路在每个设备上编译，计算开销过大。

Method: 基于图神经网络（GNN）的保真度感知调度框架：在编译前估计每个电路在每个可用QPU上的期望保真度；随后由可调调度器利用这些估计值，在电路分配时控制执行保真度与并行度之间的权衡。

Result: 该框架能够近似达到基于穷举保真度的分配效果，同时相比在每个设备上编译每个电路的暴力方法节省了计算资源。

Conclusion: GNN驱动的编译前保真度估计配合可调调度器，可在多QPU异构噪声环境下以低开销实现接近最优的保真度—并行度折中调度。

Abstract: High Performance Computing-Quantum Computing (HPCQC) platforms expose multiple Quantum Processing Units (QPUs) that may differ in size, topology, native gates, and noise characteristics. For current noisy devices, errors compound along the compiled circuits quickly, and minimizing them, that is, maximizing the circuits' execution fidelity, is essential for reliable results. Fidelity depends on the compilation to a specific target device: the same high-level circuit may produce different executables and, therefore, different expected fidelities across QPUs. We present a low-overhead fidelity-aware scheduling framework for multi-QPU systems based on a Graph Neural Network (GNN) that estimates, before compilation, the expected fidelity of each circuit on each available QPU. Then, a tunable scheduler uses these estimates to control the trade-off between execution fidelity and parallelism. Results show that this framework allows for approximating an exhaustive fidelity-based assignment, saving computational resources compared to a brute-force approach that compiles each circuit on every device.

</details>


### [77] [Direct Cultivation of Entangled $|CS\rangle$ Magic States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09993)
*Gunsik Min,Jun Heo*

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种直接培育纠缠非Clifford态 |CS⟩=CS|++⟩ 的架构，通过两个对易Clifford对合投影出四个可用分支，并利用最小六比特 [6,2,4] 记录保护分支标签。完整工厂包含 CAT₇ gadget、Steane 检错、CZZ 受控校验及 Steane-to-surface 扩展，解码使用263个操作探测比特与168个保留校验比特，采用低阶精确解析、预计算高阶目录和四陪集 BP+OSD。结果表明二阶故障内无通过闭边界逻辑失败机制，但存在三阶失效机制；优化 d=5→13 扩展可降低约35–37% 操作数，直接CS路线在较低噪声基准点更便宜，约8–9×10⁻⁴处顺序反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有魔态培育主要针对单比特非Clifford资源，缺少对纠缠非Clifford态的直接培育方案。论文旨在为 |CS⟩=CS|++⟩ 建立直接培育架构，在保护分支标签的同时实现显式可认证的三阶故障输出通道，并与三T路线进行公平比较。

Method: 利用两个对易Clifford对合将资源投影到四个由Pauli-frame更新相联系的分支；采用最小六比特 [6,2,4] 记录保护分支标签，抵御将有效记录映射为另一有效记录的读出错误。工厂由已验证CAT₇ gadget、Steane检错、基于CZZ的受控校验和立即Steane-to-surface扩展组成。解码器使用263个操作探测比特，另留168比特用于后续校验，流程包括精确低阶解析、预计算高阶目录和四陪集BP+OSD。在给定active-location随机Pauli模型下进行精确枚举、有限p仿真及三阶故障定向采样，并优化直接 d=5→13 扩展。

Result: 精确枚举显示二阶故障内不存在被接受的闭边界逻辑失败机制，但显式存在三阶失效机制。有限p仿真量化了接受率、残余综合征和解码工作量，定向采样估计了三阶故障的主导逻辑错误通道。优化直接 d=5→13 扩展使接受输出操作数降低约35–37%。在相同二进逻辑错误率下，直接CS在较低噪声基准点的操作数更少，即使三T路线被给予已有输出贴片；顺序在8×10⁻⁴与9×10⁻⁴之间反转。

Conclusion: 纠缠非Clifford态可借助受保护分支记录和显式认证的三阶故障输出通道直接培育。直接CS路线在较低噪声区间操作成本低于三T路线，但其优势在约8–9×10⁻⁴处消失并反转，说明路线选择依赖于噪声水平。

Abstract: Magic-state cultivation has so far focused mainly on single-qubit non-Clifford resources. We develop a direct cultivation architecture for the entangled state $|CS\rangle=CS|++\rangle$. Two commuting Clifford involutions project onto four usable branches related by Pauli-frame updates. A minimal six-bit $[6,2,4]$ record protects the branch label against readout errors that map one valid record to another. Verified CAT$_7$ gadgets, Steane error detection, CZZ-based controlled checks, and immediate Steane-to-surface expansion form the complete factory. The decoder uses 263 operational detector bits, while 168 additional bits are withheld for later validation. Decoding proceeds through exact low-order resolution, a precomputed higher-order catalogue, and four-coset BP+OSD. Under the stated active-location stochastic-Pauli model, exact enumeration finds no accepted closed-boundary logical-failure mechanism through fault order two, while explicit order-three failure mechanisms exist. Finite-$p$ simulations quantify acceptance, residual syndromes, and decoder workload, and targeted sampling of order-three faults estimates the leading logical-error channels. Optimizing the direct $d=5\rightarrow13$ expansion reduces accepted-output operation count by approximately 35--37\%. We compare the two routes at the same binary logical-error rate. Direct CS remains cheaper in operation count at the two lower-noise benchmark points even when the three-$T$ route is given pre-existing output patches. The ordering reverses between $8\times10^{-4}$ and $9\times10^{-4}$. That bound is the tightest that the seven of the fifteen Pauli expectation values fixing the $X$ part can support. The same theorem shows that a state has the ability whenever its $X$ part does, so partial tomography of a state can certify its ability. When noise is of $X$ form, the mixture carries a relative phase between the noise and Bell coherences that cannot be gauged away by local unitaries, and it controls the threshold through interference. Conclusion: 该工作首次对任意自旋噪声源实现半器件无关量子随机性认证，摆脱了对自旋微观模型的依赖，显著提升认证熵率并放宽输入条件，为固态系统作为可扩展高带宽量子随机数平台提供了严格认证途径。

Abstract: Complex solid-state systems are promising platforms for scalable, high-bandwidth quantum random-number generation, yet certifying the quantum origin of their fluctuations remains difficult because the underlying microscopic dynamics are hard to model and validate. Here we demonstrate semi-device-independent quantum randomness certification from semiconductor spin noise, to our knowledge the first such certificate on any spin-noise source, without relying on a microscopic model of the spin system. The untrusted optical source is constrained by an experimentally tested mean-photon-number bound together with a declared analogue-range and per-sample clipping ceiling, while the trusted receiver is described as a calibrated, noisy, coarse-grained homodyne measurement. Using a semidefinite programme with rigorously controlled Fock-space truncation, we certify randomness that remains private against an adversary holding arbitrary quantum side information. Offline analysis yields certified entropy rates of $3.2$--$3.4$\,Gbit/s from a singly charged (In,Ga)As quantum-dot ensemble and $33$\,Mbit/s from $n$-GaAs in a spin-noise-matched detection mode. This exceeds the certified entropy rate of earlier spin-noise generators by more than two orders of magnitude, and the certificate tolerates a resolved per-symbol energy contribution from the solid-state emitter itself rather than requiring a near-vacuum input.

</details>


### [82] [Quantum computational resources and validation protocols for a three-mode non-Gaussian trilinear Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10043)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯相互作用是实现连续变量量子计算普适性的关键，但其实验表征及验证其正确实现仍具挑战性，缺乏系统资源分析与高效验证手段。

Method: 聚焦三模非高斯三线性哈密顿量；用位移宇称Bell检验证明非局域态生成以操作性认证多体纠缠；量化Wigner对数负性并与既有非高斯资源态基准对比；提出结合零方差可观测量（nullifiers与stabilizers）测量与有限相空间测量的验证协议。

Result: 通过位移宇称Bell检验展示了非局域态生成，实现多体纠缠的操作性认证；量化了Wigner对数负性并完成基准比较；验证协议无需完整重构Wigner函数，大幅降低实验开销。

Conclusion: 该三线性哈密顿量可有效生成多体纠缠和Wigner负性两大关键资源，所提测量高效协议可在不重构Wigner函数条件下验证其实现，显著减少实验需求。

Abstract: Non-Gaussian interactions are a key ingredient for achieving universality in continuous-variable quantum computation, yet their experimental characterization and the validation of their correct implementation remain challenging tasks. In this work, we focus on a three-mode non-Gaussian trilinear Hamiltonian that has recently been realized in superconducting microwave platforms, and present a comprehensive theoretical analysis of the computational resources it generates, together with experimentally accessible protocols to validate their presence. We systematically investigate its ability to generate two key resources for quantum computation: multipartite entanglement and Wigner negativity. In particular, using displaced-parity Bell tests, we demonstrate the generation of nonlocal states and thereby provide an operational certification of multipartite entanglement in the non-Gaussian states produced by the dynamics. We further quantify the Wigner logarithmic negativity and benchmark it against that of established non-Gaussian resource states. Building on this resource-based characterization, we introduce a measurement-efficient protocol for the experimental validation of the Hamiltonian implementation without requiring full reconstruction of the Wigner function. The protocol combines the measurement of zero-variance observables (nullifiers and stabilizers) with a limited number of targeted phase-space measurements, leading to a drastic reduction of the experimental overhead.

</details>


### [83] [Accelerating A*-Based Algorithms for Decoding Quantum Low-Density Parity-Check Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10056)
*Lamia Yous,Francisco Garcia Herrero,Mark F. Flanagan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出两阶段BP-Tesseract混合解码框架，用BP过滤振荡比特后交由Tesseract处理残余错误，在保持逻辑错误率的同时大幅降低解码复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tesseract解码器基于A*搜索能保证找到最大似然错误模式，但实际实现中图规模极大，且搜索本质上是串行的，导致计算开销高、运行时间长。需要一种方法在保证解码性能的前提下提升效率。

Method: 两阶段解码框架：第一阶段用置信传播（BP）解码器高效处理伴随式，输出硬判决（二进制错误向量）和软信息（每比特置信度）；在BP不收敛时，通过门控机制检测并滤除置信度振荡的比特，将精炼后的输出作为Tesseract的输入，由Tesseract搜索并纠正残余错误。

Result: 在所有测试的物理错误率下，Tesseract展开节点数至少减少5倍，在[[126,12,d<11]] T1码、p=0.05时峰值减少近15倍，在[[72,12,6]] BB码、p=0.05时约减少8.8倍，同时保持与单独使用Tesseract相同的逻辑错误率（LER）。

Conclusion: 该混合方法利用BP的高速性并将困难实例交给Tesseract，在维持逻辑错误率不变的情况下显著降低整体解码复杂度，验证了BP预处理与门控过滤对加速A*类解码的有效性。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes represent a promising approach for error correction in quantum computing. The recently proposed Tesseract decoder uses the A* search algorithm that guarantees finding the most likely error pattern. However, practical implementations of Tesseract often involve an extremely large graph, and the inherently sequential nature of the search results in high computational overhead and long runtime. To improve decoding efficiency, we propose a two-stage decoding framework. First, a belief propagation (BP) decoder efficiently processes the syndrome. This step generates hard decisions (a binary error vector) and soft information (per-qubit confidence levels). In non-convergent BP cases, a gating mechanism examines the BP decoder's output to identify and filter out qubits with oscillating confidence values. In these cases, the refined output serves as input to Tesseract, which then attempts to identify and correct any residual errors. This hybrid approach leverages the high speed of BP decoding and delegates the more challenging decoding instances to Tesseract. Numerical results demonstrate a substantial reduction in overall decoding complexity while maintaining the logical error rate (LER) of the stand-alone Tesseract. Across all tested physical error rates, the proposed method achieves at least 5x reduction in the number of expanded nodes in the Tesseract, with a peak reduction of nearly 15x at a physical error rate of p = 0.05 for the [[126, 12, d < 11]] T1 code, and approximately 8.8x at p = 0.05 for the [[72, 12, 6]] bicycle bivariate (BB) code.

</details>


### [84] [Haar-Bayesian Pure-State Prediction under Relative-Entropy Loss: Arbitrary-Effect Reduction and Global Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10072)
*Masahito Hayashi,Ayanava Dasgupta,Naqueeb Ahmad Warsi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在观测 n 个未知纯态副本并施加任意集体测量后，对未测量副本的 Haar-Bayesian 预测问题，以量子相对熵衡量性能，并给出全局最优测量与其精确风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子态学习与预测中，如何对未观测副本进行最优预测是一个核心问题。作者希望在任意集体测量范围内寻找使 Bayes 预测损失最小的测量方案，同时刻画其 minimax 性质。

Method: 采用 Haar 先验下的贝叶斯预测框架，以量子相对熵为损失。固定测量时，最优预测态为后验均值，条件损失为其熵；随后在所有对称子空间上的 POVM 中优化。对任意非零正效应 E，推导出后验预测态 μ_E=(I+nρ_E)/(n+d)，其中 ρ_E 为 E 的归一化单粒子边缘；利用纯态谱主化任意密度算符谱得到逐结果熵下界。相干秩一效应用达到该下界，其 Haar 轨道给出最高权协变 POVM。

Result: 最高权协变 POVM 在所有集体测量中全局 Bayes 最优，并由协变性知全局 minimax。其精确风险为 h_d((n+1)/(n+d))，其中 h_d(r)=-r log r-(1-r)log((1-r)/(d-1))。同一任意效应约化还表明该 POVM 最大化潜在纯态与后验预测态之间的联合重叠（等价于平均后验纯度），最优值为 ((n+1)^2+d-1)/(n+d)^2。

Conclusion: 存在一个统一的最优集体测量——最高权协变 POVM，它同时实现最小编码预测风险与最大后验纯度，为纯态贝叶斯预测问题提供了闭式最优解。

Abstract: We study Haar-Bayesian prediction of one unmeasured copy of an unknown finite-dimensional pure quantum state after an arbitrary collective measurement on $n$ observed copies. Performance is evaluated by quantum relative entropy. For a fixed measurement, the Bayes predictive state is the posterior mean and the optimized conditional loss is its entropy. We then optimize the measurement over all POVMs on the symmetric subspace. For every nonzero positive effect $E$, the corresponding posterior predictive state is $μ_E=(I+nρ_E)/(n+d)$, where $ρ_E$ is the normalized one-particle marginal of $E$. Since a pure spectrum majorizes every density-operator spectrum, this identity gives an outcome-wise entropy lower bound. Coherent rank-one effects attain the bound, and their Haar orbit yields the highest-weight covariant POVM. Hence this POVM is globally Bayes optimal over all collective measurements and, by covariance, globally minimax. Its exact risk is $h_d((n+1)/(n+d))$, where $h_d(r)=-r\log r-(1-r)\log((1-r)/(d-1))$. The same arbitrary-effect reduction shows that the highest-weight POVM also maximizes the joint overlap between the latent pure state and its posterior predictive state, equivalently the mean posterior purity, with optimum $((n+1)^2+d-1)/(n+d)^2$.

</details>


### [85] [Why three? A two-level system with four mutually unbiased questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10078)
*Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 关于两级系统中无偏问句（互不偏基）数量的算子代数结构研究：四个反交换对易式对应超比特（四维Bloch球）模型，作者刻画其缺失性质并在双量子比特中实现。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两问句相互无偏当且仅当其对合算子反交换，因此一组无偏问句构成Clifford代数，其最大规模为奇数，导致实数、复数、四元数量子理论分别允许2、3、5个问句而跳过4个。作者欲探究在被舍弃的四问句情形下，单系统Jordan态空间公理能允许多大程度的模型，以及该模型缺少什么、能否嵌入更大的量子系统。

Method: 将无偏问句的对合算子表述为Clifford代数与反交换算子积约束；构造四维Bloch球（超比特）模型；分析其Jordan代数/态空间结构，指出仅"能量可观测性"能排除它；在双量子比特系统中显式实现该模型，考察其Kirkwood-Dirac虚部（作为隐藏的so(4)生成元）、复合系统纠缠与相互作用、24-cell态的Spekkens制备语境性，并给出费米子表述（四个问句为两模的Majorana算符，第五个为宇称）。

Result: 两级系统在无对合算子积约束下存在四问句模型即四维Bloch球（超比特）；单系统公理允许该模型，唯有能量可观测性将其排除。该模型在双量子比特中可实现：其Kirkwood-Dirac虚部为隐藏的so(4)生成元；其复合系统允许纠缠但无相互作用；其24-cell态在去极化强度低至2/3时仍保持Spekkens制备语境性。费米子图像中，四问句为两模的Majorana算符，第五问句为宇称，故四问句系统即被宇称超选择移除的扇区。

Abstract: Two sharp yes/no questions are mutually unbiased if and only if their involutions anticommute. A set of unbiased questions is therefore a Clifford algebra, whose maximal size is odd, meaning four anticommuting questions typically imply a fifth: the two-level systems of real, complex and quaternionic quantum theory allow two, three and five questions, while four is skipped. Without this operator product restriction, a two-level system with four unbiased questions exists as the four-dimensional Bloch ball, the hyperbit. The single-system postulates that pick out the Jordan state spaces allow such a model, and only energy observability rules it out. We show what it lacks, and realise it inside two qubits: its Kirkwood-Dirac imaginary parts are hidden $\mathfrak{so}(4)$ generators, its composites allow entanglement but no interaction, and its 24-cell of states stays Spekkens preparation contextual down to depolarising strength $2/3$. In fermionic terms, the four questions are the Majorana operators of two modes while the fifth is parity: the four-question system is therefore the sector removed by parity superselection.

</details>

Result: 相较分区（zoned）基线，每轮综合提取的累积丢失最多改善2.15倍；相较当前架构，逻辑错误率降低超过两个数量级。该框架还为连续重载速率、AOD数量与穿梭轨迹选择等具体器件目标提供了指导。

A dominant error source on this platform is qubit loss, which accrues with every operation and movement. The presence of loss undermines the promises of existing architectural work. Standard error correction targets stochastic Pauli errors and cannot correct loss, so most FTQC performance analyses are not directly compatible with it. Moreover, compilation and routing decisions, which strongly affect overall loss, are typically optimized against Pauli-error cost models and often remain loss-agnostic, potentially increasing exposure to the loss channel.

We target a physical implementation with no separate storage and entangling zones, eliminating the repeated SLM-AOD handoffs and long-distance shuttling that dominate loss in other layouts. Overall, we improve accumulated loss per syndrome-extraction round by up to 2.15X against a zoned baseline and reduce logical error rates by over two orders of magnitude versus current architectures. We expect these insights to matter for system architects as neutral-atom hardware scales.

Abstract: Photon subtraction is one of the most important techniques for generating non-Gaussian optical states and constitutes a key resource for quantum information processing and quantum metrology. For simultaneous measurement of parameters, we evaluate the sloppiness of the underlying statistical model and analyze its dependence on the measured quadrature, probe photon number, squeezing fraction, beam splitter transmissivity, and detector efficiency. Our analysis proves that an appropriate choice of the homodyne quadrature substantially reduces parameter degeneracy and enables efficient simultaneous estimation. Furthermore, we show that the joint estimation strategy consistently provides a lower estimation bound than the sequential estimation approach over a broad range of experimentally relevant parameters.

Abstract: Entanglement-assisted optical interferometry promises diffraction-limited imaging over baselines where light cannot be physically combined, but existing theory treats only a single visibility on one two-station baseline. This work formulates the multiparameter estimation problem for an M-station array imaging an extended scene. A reduction lemma shows that the multimode quantum Fisher information (QFI) equals the mean photon number times the QFI of a single delocalized photon, collapsing the problem to a finite-dimensional one. The local photon-number superselection rule (SSR) erases all phase information from the bare state, while shared entanglement restores a fraction f(r)=r/(1+r) of the QFI on a baseline supplied with r pairs -- a factor shown to be achievable, since the reduced two-mode state of any pair is exactly the two-station weak-thermal state. The QFI matrix and its mean Uhlmann curvature reveal numerically that a point source is measurement-compatible, whereas for every extended-source model examined it is incompatible, the Holevo bound exceeding the symmetric-logarithmic-derivative (SLD) bound by up to ~74%. An explicit collective receiver -- a global mode-sorting (quantum Fourier transform) measurement -- then attains a weighted variance within ~7% of the SLD bound and roughly an order of magnitude below an explicit pairwise receiver, and a noisy-resource advantage threshold gives the break-even baseline (~20 km for near-term parameters) beyond which repeater-distributed entanglement beats direct transmission. Finally, the optimal allocation of a finite entanglement budget is a convex program with a closed-form proportional (square-root-law) solution favoring low-visibility baselines. These results recast quantum aperture synthesis as collective multiparameter estimation and give concrete design targets, illustrated on the CHARA array.

</details>

Abstract: How many past requests are needed to decide which qubits should share entanglement? We show that the answer depends on the allocation choices created by the queries: a larger memory can require no more data. The memory stores a classical bit and answers requests through a fixed detector that preserves coherence within each measured sector. For independent commuting $X$- and $Z$-type Pauli queries, we characterize the full attainable prediction-contrast region and construct encodings that preserve the bit at every nonzero vertex. With sharp reports, a $d$-qubit path and groups of at most $k$ qubits have minimax excess error after $m$ requests proportional to $k^{-1}\min\{1,\sqrt{d\log(k+1)/m}\}$, uniformly for $2\leq k<d$. Connected biclique regions can grow without increasing sample demand when depth, region count and connections per region stay bounded. Preparation noise introduces a separate calibration requirement. We derive an exact tradeoff with extra fresh detector calls and transfer the learning law to structured transaction co-location. Population-risk experiments test the statistical predictions. We also compare encodings on a native 15-qubit device and learned partitions on public purchase baskets. The full chain wins on the device; frequency grouping outperforms basket search in the largest-capacity retail setting.

</details>

*Maximilian Reichert,Javier Navarro,Gerardo Adesso,Manuel Gessner,Mikel Sanz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于非对称右除超算符的量子Barankin界，用于量子参数估计，可对任意偏置约束下的估计量均方误差给出下界，并获得高斯态Barankin矩阵的解析表达式，能有效计算该界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Cramér-Rao界是量子参数估计的标准精度极限工具，但其在少样本（few-shot）区域无法刻画阈值效应，导致精度评估不完整；已有量子Barankin界基于对称除超算符，难以获得高斯态等情形下的解析结果。因此需要一种可施加全局无偏性等任意偏置约束、且便于解析计算的替代下界。

Result: 给出了量子Barankin界及其基于非对称右除超算符的表述，获得高斯态下Barankin矩阵的解析表达式，使该界可被高效计算；并通过多个呈现少样本阈值效应的示例验证了其有效性，而这些阈值效应在标准量子Cramér-Rao界方法中不可见。

Conclusion: 所提出的量子Barankin界可作为量子Cramér-Rao界的有效补充，能够刻画全局无偏估计的精度极限并揭示少样本阈值效应；其非对称右除超算符表述使高斯态的Barankin矩阵具有解析形式，便于实际计算与应用。

Abstract: We propose a quantum version of the Barankin bound as an alternative to the quantum Cramér-Rao bound for quantum parameter estimation. The quantum Barankin bound provides a lower bound on the mean squared error of estimators satisfying arbitrarily chosen bias constraints at arbitrarily chosen parameter points. In particular, unbiasedness over the entire parameter space can be imposed, yielding precision limits for globally unbiased quantum parameter estimation. In contrast to the recently derived quantum Barankin bound, which is based on a symmetric division superoperator, our bound is based on a nonsymmetric right-division superoperator. This formulation enables us to find analytic expressions for the Barankin matrix for Gaussian states, which allows for an efficient calculation of the bound. We demonstrate the usefulness of our results by applying them to various examples that exhibit the threshold effect in the few-shot regime, which is invisible to the standard quantum Cramér-Rao bound approach.

</details>


### [91] [Unbounded degree overhead for Alice-conditioned quantum Bell certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10162)
*Fumin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Alice条件化NPA层级（每个平方和项仅涉及Alice的一个测量问题）的认证成本问题。在最简单Bell场景中证明了不存在任何有限层级能包含全部标准二级Bell证书，且所需条件化层级至少以(2-α)^{-1/2}增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 要求每个sum-of-squares项只涉及Alice的一个测量问题可能导致无界的认证成本，作者希望量化这种条件化结构所带来的层级代价，并厘清它与普通SOS次数之间的本质区别。

Abstract: Requiring each sum-of-squares term to involve only one of Alice's measurement questions can impose an unbounded certification cost. In the simplest Bell scenario, we prove that no finite level of the Alice-conditioned NPA hierarchy contains all standard level-two Bell certificates. An explicit family of truncated positive functionals on the infinite dihedral group exceeds the tilted-CHSH quantum bound at every prescribed finite level, while a standard degree-two certificate is exact. A Fejer-weighted trace reduces positivity to a rank-one subtraction from a moving-average Gram matrix. The required conditioned level grows at least as $(2-α)^{-1/2}$ near the endpoint tilt. This bounds the degree of exact nice-SOS inputs to compiled-game soundness proofs. Consequently, no finite conditioned level certifies the entire optimal CHSH randomness tradeoff against quantum side information, although standard level two does. Away from the endpoint, we prove a sharp one-level cost throughout $α\in[13/10,3/2]$, using optimal-strategy kernels and exact Bernstein matrix positivity to certify a continuous interval. The results separate ordinary SOS degree from the resources keyed by single-question certificate structure.

</details>


### [92] [Finite-Particle Quantum Reduction of Thermodynamic Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10211)
*Borhan Ahmadi*
