  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学能在无法进行微观重建时，仅凭少数可及宏观量预测和控制复杂系统。作者追问：这种操作层面的约简，在有限量子系统中是否比在与之匹配的经典系统中代价更低。

Method: 将少体玻色-哈伯德链与保持粒子数的经典场对应物进行对比，在相同驱动和相同粗粒化空间记录条件下考察两者的弛豫与信息保留。随后把过程闭合为一个热机循环，其控制仅依赖测得的平均能量与空间记录，而不需要完整量子态层析。

Result: 量子系统在观测到的粒子分布上弛豫更充分，其保留的平均能量解析出更多被粗粒记录隐藏的微观结构，导致不可用信息更少、熵产生更小；在匹配热资源时，更小的熵产生带来更多功和更高效率。该优势随粒子数增加而减弱，逐渐趋向经典场情形。

Conclusion: 有限量子系统在热力学操作约简上可能比经典对应物更高效：以少量可测量量实现控制时，量子系统信息浪费更少、熵产生更低、热机性能更优，但这一量子优势会随粒子数增长而消退。

Abstract: Thermodynamics is useful because it lets us predict and control complex systems from a few accessible quantities when microscopic reconstruction is impractical. We ask whether this operational reduction can be less costly in a finite quantum system than in a matched classical one. We compare a few-boson Bose--Hubbard chain with its number-conserving classical-field counterpart under the same driving and the same coarse spatial record. The quantum system relaxes more in the observed particle distribution, yet the retained mean energy resolves more of the microscopic structure hidden by that record, leaving less information unusable and smaller entropy generation. We then close the process into a heat-engine cycle whose controls use only the measured mean energy and spatial record, not full state tomography; at matched thermal resources, the smaller entropy generation gives more work and higher efficiency. The advantage fades as the particle number grows toward the classical-field regime.

</details>


### [93] [Information capacity of quantum statistics: Fock-state tests of a discrete binary-sequence model on cloud photonic quantum processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10216)
*Chiran Wijesundara,Octavia T. Volpe,Dejan Stojkovic,Herbert Fotso,Tim Thomay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用商用云光子量子处理器，对离散序列模型（量子力学的统计极限理论）进行高精度检验，将有限信息容量 n 下界约束至 n>100，并展示硬件可实现 n~10^3 量级的检验灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文核心出发点是：量子力学可能只是某种更基本离散理论的统计极限。该类理论给物理系统赋予有限信息容量，系统偏离量子统计的程度由其容量使用比例决定。因此需要实验上界定这一容量，判断量子统计究竟有多接近完备。

Method: 以 Powers 等人的二元序列模型作为具体检验理论：结果概率来自长度为 n 的离散序列计数，n→∞时恢复量子力学，n 即制备态背后寄存器的信息容量。在可编程干涉仪上测量光子 Fock 态 |1⟩、|1,1⟩、预报 |2⟩及级联分束器对，并原位确定主导系统误差。采用要求旋转可复合的复合一致参数化，用参数自助法校准的随机效应似然分析排除有限 n；级联分束器用于直接检验复合律。

Result: 复合一致参数化以偏差 1.24/n 恢复量子力学；随机效应似然分析排除所有 n≤100，即双光子态寄存器若容量有限则超过 10^2。级联分束器数据呈分束不变性，以 8σ 排除朴素计数复合，并确认干涉符号规则。模型无关地，曲线平均偏离量子配分律大于 2.3×10^-2 被 95% 置信水平排除，原发表的线性参数化被直接排除。

Conclusion: 云光子处理器已具备检验量子力学底层离散性的定量精度；由于编译偏移按电路冻结、可校准，当前硬件即可打开 10^-3 精度下限（n~10^3）。因此云光子处理器可作为量子基础研究的定量仪器，信息容量成为可实验约束的物理量。

Abstract: Our central premise is that quantum mechanics may be the statistical limit of a more fundamental discrete theory: any such theory equips a physical system with a finite information capacity, and its departure from quantum statistics is controlled by how much of that capacity the system uses. We show that commercial cloud photonic quantum processors have reached the precision required to bound this capacity from below, using the binary-sequence model of Powers et al. as the concrete test theory: outcome probabilities arise from counting discrete sequences of length $n$, quantum mechanics is recovered as $n \to \infty$, and $n$ measures the information capacity of the register behind a prepared state. Photon Fock states $|1\rangle$, $|1,1\rangle$, heralded $|2\rangle$, and cascaded beam-splitter pairs are measured on programmable interferometers with dominant systematics determined in situ. The model's composition-consistent parametrization, singled out by requiring that rotations compose, recovers quantum mechanics with deviations $1.24/n$; a random-effects likelihood analysis calibrated by parametric bootstrap excludes all $n \le 100$: the information capacity of the register carrying the two-photon state, if finite, exceeds $10^2$. Cascaded beam splitters test the composition law directly: the data are split-invariant, excluding naive count composition at $8σ$ and confirming the interference-sign rule. Model-independently, curve-averaged deviations from the quantum partition law larger than $2.3\times10^{-2}$ are excluded at 95% CL, and the originally published linear parametrization is excluded outright. Because the compilation offset is frozen per circuit it is calibratable, opening the $10^{-3}$ floor ($n \sim 10^3$) to current hardware: cloud photonic processors are quantitative instruments for quantum foundations, and information capacity an experimentally boundable quantity.

</details>


### [94] [An analysis of iterative refinement for quantum linear system solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10250)
*Adrian Harkness,Mohammadhossein Mohammadisiahroudi,Brandon Augustino,Giacomo Nannicini,Tamás Terlaky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出迭代精化(IR)框架，使结合量子线性系统算法(QLSA)与量子态层析的算法在全程保持对逆误差容限的多对数依赖，并在模拟器与真实量子硬件上验证其有效性与抗噪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QLSA虽能以多对数复杂度制备编码解的量子态，但通过层析提取经典描述时会引入对目标精度的多项式依赖，破坏了整体的多对数精度依赖特性。因此需要一种方法在提取高精度经典解的过程中避免这一多项式开销。

Method: 提出迭代精化(IR)方案：通过求解一系列相关线性系统逐步精化解，全程仅需固定精度的量子子程序，最终获得高精度解的经典描述。在三种输入模型下进行分析：量子读/经典写RAM(QRAM)、酉线性组合(LCU)以及稀疏访问预言机；并通过量子模拟器与真实量子硬件的数值实验进行评估。

Result: 数值实验表明，迭代精化能高效提升解的精度，并对硬件噪声表现出鲁棒性；在QRAM、LCU和稀疏访问预言机三种输入模型下框架分析均成立。

Conclusion: 迭代精化框架可有效解决QLSA结合层析时精度依赖退化的问题，在保持多对数复杂度的同时获得高精度经典解，且具备硬件噪声鲁棒性，适用于多种输入模型。

Abstract: We present and analyze an iterative refinement (IR) framework for improving the precision dependence of algorithms that combine a quantum linear system algorithm (QLSA) with quantum state tomography. Existing QLSAs achieve polylogarithmic dependence on the inverse error tolerance to prepare a quantum state encoding the solution, but extracting a classical description of such a state via tomography typically introduces a polynomial dependence on the target precision. To retain polylogarithmic dependence in the inverse error tolerance throughout the entire process, we develop an IR scheme that solves a sequence of related linear systems to progressively refine the solution while requiring only fixed-precision quantum subroutines, finally obtaining a classical description of a high-precision solution. We analyze the proposed framework under three input models: quantum-read/classical-write RAM (QRAM), linear combinations of unitaries (LCU), and sparse-access oracles. We evaluate the proposed scheme through numerical experiments on both quantum simulators and real quantum hardware. The results demonstrate that iterative refinement efficiently improves the precision of the solution and exhibits robustness to hardware noise.

</details>


### [95] [Solving wave propagation problems via geometric quantum state preparation on dispersion manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10282)
*Yakov Solomons,Lee Peleg,Netanel Barel,Jonathan Nemirovsky,Amit Ben-Kish,Yotam Shapira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子算法求解频域Helmholtz方程，通过在色散流形上直接制备量子态并利用傅里叶相位编码源位置，消除对共振流形后选择的指数级开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子线性系统求解器可指数压缩系统自由度，但运行复杂度受离散算子条件数支配；利用微分算子的解析结构可避免显式矩阵求逆。然而Helmholtz方程的逆算子在色散面k²=ω²/c²上奇异，导致直接傅里叶空间态制备方法指数低效。

Method: 以线性系统形式求解偏微分方程，聚焦频域离散Helmholtz方程描述的波动传播问题；借鉴屏蔽泊松方程通过显式量子电路利用算子解析结构的思路，针对Helmholtz方程直接在共振流形上制备量子态，并通过傅里叶相位编码源位置。

Result: 消除了共振流形后选择带来的指数级大开销，算法成功概率与源的数量呈线性关系，且与计算域尺寸无关。

Conclusion: 该方法可推广到傅里叶空间解在色散流形上具有奇异支撑的双曲型微分方程，将其求解重新表述为几何量子态制备问题。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm for solving partial differential equations through a linear-system formulation, focusing on wave propagation problems described by the discretized Helmholtz equation in frequency domain. Although quantum linear-system solvers offer exponential compression of the system degrees of freedom, their runtime complexity is generally governed by the condition number of the discretized operator. Exploiting the analytic structure of the differential operator can provide an alternative to explicit matrix inversion, as illustrated for the screened Poisson equation through an explicit quantum circuit. For the Helmholtz equation, however, the inverse operator becomes singular on the dispersion surface $k^2=ω^2/c^2$, rendering direct Fourier-space state-preparation methods exponentially inefficient. We address this challenge by directly preparing quantum states supported on the resonant manifold and encoding source locations through Fourier phases. The resulting algorithm eliminates the exponentially large overhead associated with post-selection on the resonant manifold, yielding a success probability that depends linearly on the number of sources and is independent of the computational domain size. More generally, our approach applies to hyperbolic differential equations whose Fourier-space solutions possess singular support on dispersion manifolds, recasting their solution as a problem of geometric quantum state preparation.

</details>


### [96] [Programmable photonic state fusion via heralded storage of asynchronously generated resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10312)
*Mustafa Gündoğan,Dennis Rätzel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种融合协议，通过主动存储环路将独立预报的光子态依次加载到同一循环模式中，消除其产生时间标签，实现可编程叠加态的构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率性光子源在不同试验中产生基本态，而多光子协议要求它们在共同的时间模式中干涉，二者存在不匹配问题。

Method: 将独立预报的光子态依次加载到主动存储环路；在监测的dump模式中条件化于真空，选择每个新态被转移到与已存储光子相同循环模式的事件，从而去除生成时间标签。利用各基本态两种备选经历相同加载变换的性质，使累积态表示为可编程线性因子的乘积，通过多项式因式分解构建目标叠加态；并随态增长调整存储环路耦合。

Result: 调整存储环路耦合显著提高加载效率，将固定平衡耦合器的快于指数惩罚改为指数标度；并将该协议应用于光子时钟干涉测量的双光子路径—频率态，估计了包含源等待时间和往返损耗的探测速率。

Conclusion: 该融合协议可克服概率源与多光子协议的时间模式不匹配，为可编程光子叠加态的构建及光子时钟干涉测量应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic photonic sources generate elementary states in different trials, whereas multiphoton protocols require them to interfere in common temporal modes. We propose a fusion protocol that overcomes this mismatch by successively loading independently heralded photonic states into active storage loops. Conditioning on vacuum in monitored dump modes selects events in which each newly generated state is transferred into the same circulating modes as the photons already stored, thereby removing its generation time label. Provided the two alternatives of each elementary state undergo the same loading transformation, the accumulated state is described by a product of programmable linear factors, allowing a target superposition to be constructed by polynomial factorization. Adjusting the storage loop coupling as the state grows substantially improves the loading efficiency, changing the faster-than-exponential penalty of fixed balanced couplers to exponential scaling. We apply the protocol to two-photon path--frequency states for photonic clock interferometry and estimate the detected rate including source waiting time and round-trip loss.

</details>


### [97] [Constant-depth global shadow estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10408)
*Qingyue Zhang,Zhou You,Dayue Qin,Xiaopeng Li,Jens Eisert,You Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出浅层相位阴影（shallow phase shadows），基于稀疏 Clifford-IQP 系综，在系综不构成近似相对误差设计的情况下，实现对稳定子态保真度的高效全局估计，并给出常数深度和対数深度的实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子处理器规模扩大，读出（readout）环节不能成为主要瓶颈，需要可靠且可扩展的读出策略。随机测量与经典阴影提供了强大途径，但全局估计通常依赖高度随机的系综，需要不断增加的电路深度，带来显著的实验开销。

Method: 作者提出浅层相位阴影，基于稀疏的 Clifford-IQP 系综。该系综并非近似相对误差设计，但作者证明其仍可对稳定子态保真度进行高效全局估计。在全连接架构上，方案可用中途测量加经典前馈实现常数深度，或无辅助系统时实现対数深度；协议仅需受控相位纠缠门，并提供电路资源与估计精度之间的可调权衡。

Result: 证明了浅层相位阴影对稳定子态保真度实现高效全局估计；在 all-to-all 架构上实现常数深度（借助中途测量与经典前馈）或対数深度（无辅助系统）；仅需受控相位门即可完成，并具有电路资源与精度的可调折中。

Conclusion: 可扩展的量子读出不必复现通用随机性：面向任务的适应性随机化能够实现显著更浅的全局表征协议，特别适合具有长程连接、实验上相关的架构。

Abstract: Reliable and scalable readout strategies are essential for quantum technologies. As quantum processors grow, extracting useful information must remain feasible without measurement circuits becoming a dominant bottleneck. Randomized measurements and classical shadows provide a powerful route, but global estimation is conventionally associated with highly random ensembles that require increasing circuit depth and hence substantial experimental overhead. In this work, we show that substantially less randomness suffices when the readout is meaningfully adapted to the quantities being estimated. We introduce shallow phase shadows, based on a sparse Clifford-IQP ensemble, and prove efficient global estimation of stabilizer-state fidelities despite the ensemble not forming an approximate relative-error design. On all-to-all architectures, the protocol admits a constant-depth implementation using mid-circuit measurements and classical feedforward, or logarithmic depth without auxiliary systems. The protocol requires only controlled-phase entangling gates and offers a tunable trade-off between circuit resources and estimation accuracy, making it particularly amenable to experimentally relevant architectures with long-range connectivity. Our results show that scalable quantum readout need not reproduce generic randomness: task-adapted randomization can enable substantially shallower global characterization protocols.

</details>


### [98] [Faster Computation with the Generalized Laplacian Quantum Walk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10429)
*Jonas Duda,Thomas G. Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明由广义拉普拉斯算子驱动的连续时间量子行走在完全二部图上搜索多个标记顶点时，其时间可逼近最优，且快于标准拉普拉斯或邻接矩阵驱动的典型量子行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子行走是量子计算的通用模型并支撑多种量子算法，但典型量子行走（由标准拉普拉斯算子或邻接矩阵驱动）在搜索问题上的效率存在提升空间，作者希望借助源自自旋链的广义拉普拉斯算子来加速搜索。

Method: 采用连续时间量子行走框架，将演化算子取为广义拉普拉斯算子，即标准拉普拉斯算子加上度矩阵的实数倍；分析该行走在完全二部图上搜索多个标记顶点的行为，并研究度矩阵倍数增大时搜索时间的变化。

Result: 随着度矩阵实数倍数幅度的增大，对应量子行走搜索完全二部图上多个标记顶点所需时间趋近于最优；该广义拉普拉斯量子行走比标准拉普拉斯或邻接矩阵驱动的典型量子行走更快地解决该计算问题。

Conclusion: 广义拉普拉斯量子行走有潜力成为一种开发更多更快量子算法的有用方法。

Abstract: Quantum walks are the quantum analogues of classical random walks or Markov chains. They are universal models of quantum computing, and they underpin a variety of quantum algorithms. We prove that a continuous-time quantum walk effected by a generalized Laplacian, which can arise in spin chains, can solve a computational problem more quickly than typical quantum walks governed by the standard Laplacian or adjacency matrix. This generalized Laplacian consists of the standard Laplacian plus a real-valued multiple of the degree matrix, and we prove that as the magnitude of the multiple of the degree matrix is increased, its corresponding quantum walk can search the complete bipartite graph with multiple marked vertices in time that approaches the optimal. This raises the potential for the generalized Laplacian quantum walk to be a useful method for developing additional faster quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [99] [Coherent advantage in the computational expressivity of excitonic networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10448)
*Matthew Du,Carlos Floyd,Dipti Jasrasaria,Suriyanarayanan Vaikuntanathan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究驱动-耗散激子网络作为可训练计算平台，发现量子相干性使计算表达能力随网络规模扩展，类似人工神经网络，而强退相干会抑制这种扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI能耗上升促使人们寻找物理系统作为可训练计算的替代基底；近期实验已能精确调控分子发色团间耦合，产生相干激发动力学，为利用激子网络进行计算提供了可能。

Method: 将驱动-耗散激子网络作为计算平台，以格点间耦合作为输入，稳态作为输出，分析其表达能力随网络规模的扩展行为，并考察退相干强度的影响。

Result: 相干性使计算表达能力随网络规模扩展，行为类似人工神经网络；强退相干同时抑制这种规模扩展和整体表达能力。

Conclusion: 相干性是使非平衡量子系统具备高表达能力计算的资源，确立了激子网络作为可训练计算平台的前景。

Abstract: The rising energy consumption of AI has generated interest in physical systems as alternative substrates for trainable computation. Recent experimental advances have enabled precise control over the couplings between molecular chromophores, which give rise to coherent excitation dynamics. Here, we study driven-dissipative excitonic networks as a computational platform, where the intersite couplings define the input and the steady state defines the output. We show that coherence enables computational expressivity to scale with network size, analogous to artificial neural networks. Both this scaling and the overall expressivity are suppressed by strong dephasing. Our work establishes coherence as a resource for expressive computation in nonequilibrium quantum systems.

</details>


### [100] [Information Causality Characterizes the Set of Quantum Correlations in the Simplest Bell Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10508)
*Mariami Gachechiladze,Nikolai Miklin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文结合信息因果律（IC）的广义表述与新的通信协议，直接推导出刻画最简二体贝尔场景（两个二值测量）量子关联的贝尔不等式，即Tsirelson-Landau-Masanes判据，并证明广义IC严格强于宏观局域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息因果律作为非信号理论中约束关联的物理原理，能否精确恢复量子关联边界（超越Uffink不等式）一直是悬而未决的问题。作者希望厘清广义IC与量子关联集、宏观局域性之间的关系，并强化IC在解释量子非局域性极限中的作用。

Method: 将信息因果律的广义形式（针对关联输入）与一种新的通信协议相结合，用于在最简二体贝尔场景中推导量子贝尔不等式。

Result: 直接从IC推导出Tsirelson-Landau-Masanes判据；证明广义IC蕴含宏观局域性，同时给出违反广义IC但满足宏观局域性的关联实例，表明广义IC在该场景中严格更强。

Conclusion: 广义IC严格强于宏观局域性，结合此前"广义IC蕴含非平凡通信复杂度原理"的结果，进一步确立了IC在解释量子非局域性极限中的核心地位，并为在更一般贝尔场景中推导更紧的量子关联边界提供了系统途径。

Abstract: Information causality (IC) was introduced as a physical principle constraining correlations in non-signaling theories. Whether it can recover the exact quantum correlation boundary, beyond Uffink's inequality, has remained an open question. Here, we combine its generalized formulation for correlated inputs with a new communication protocol to derive quantum Bell inequalities that exactly characterize the quantum correlations in the simplest bipartite Bell scenario, with two binary measurements. In particular, we derive the Tsirelson-Landau-Masanes criterion directly from IC. Thus, the generalized IC implies macroscopic locality, while we also present macroscopically local correlations that violate the generalized IC. This establishes that generalized IC is a strictly stronger principle in this scenario. Together with our earlier result that generalized IC implies a nontrivial communication complexity principle, these findings strengthen the role of information causality in explaining the limits of quantum nonlocality and provide a systematic route to deriving tighter bounds on the set of quantum correlations in more general Bell scenarios.

</details>


### [101] [Optimal Low-Rank Quantum State Tomography with Bounded-Sample Joint Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10514)
*Ashwin Nayak,Xingyu Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了低秩量子态层析在允许至多 $t$ 个样本联合测量时的最优样本复杂度，为 $\Theta\left(\frac{dr}{\varepsilon^2}\max\{1,r/\sqrt t\}\right)$，并证明联合测量相对单样本测量最多只能带来 $\sqrt t$ 倍加速，联合测量需约 $r^2$ 个样本才能达到无限制集体测量速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究联合测量（作用于多个样本）对低秩量子态层析样本复杂度的提升上限，明确最优样本复杂度及联合测量规模的收益边界，并刻画达到无限制集体速率所需的联合样本数。

Method: 下界：固定均匀谱、变化状态支撑，界定任意 $t$ 样本联合测量的 Fisher 信息迹，结合自适应 Fisher 链式法则与 van Trees 不等式导出迹范数下界。上界：构造并分析基于高斯联合测量的非自适应层析协议，利用显式二阶矩恒等式及状态支撑外的条件高斯律进行秩相关误差分析。

Result: 样本复杂度精确为 $\Theta\left(\frac{dr}{\varepsilon^2}\max\{1,r/\sqrt t\}\right)$；下界对自适应联合测量均成立，上界为非自适应且匹配；联合测量相对单样本测量至多提升 $\sqrt t$ 倍；联合测量约 $r^2$ 个样本是达到无限制集体速率的充要条件。

Conclusion: 联合测量对低秩量子态层析的样本复杂度提升受限于 $\sqrt t$ 因子，且需联合约 $r^2$ 个样本方可实现无限制集体测量速率；该结论给出了该问题的紧致刻画。

Abstract: We determine the optimal sample complexity of low-rank quantum state tomography when each measurement may act jointly on at most $t$ samples. For sufficiently small $\varepsilon$, estimating an unknown state on $\mathbb{C}^d$ of rank at most $r$ to trace norm error $\varepsilon$ with constant success probability requires, and is achievable with, $$
  Θ\left(
  \frac{dr}{\varepsilon^2}
  \max\left\{1,\frac r{\sqrt t}\right\}
  \right)$$ samples. The lower bound allows the protocol to choose each joint measurement adaptively using all previous classical outcomes; the matching upper bound is nonadaptive. Thus joint measurements on at most $t$ samples improve the complexity of algorithms making single-sample measurements by at most a factor $\sqrt t$. Further, measuring order $r^2$ samples jointly is necessary and sufficient to attain the unrestricted collective rate.
  For the lower bound, we vary the support of a state with fixed uniform spectrum and bound the Fisher information trace of every joint measurement on $t$ samples. The adaptive Fisher chain rule and the van Trees inequality then give the trace norm lower bound. For the upper bound, we construct and analyze a nonadaptive tomography protocol based on a Gaussian joint measurement. An explicit second moment identity and a conditional Gaussian law outside the state's support give a rank-dependent error analysis, yielding the matching rate.

</details>


### [102] [Private communication via zero-private-capacity quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10520)
*Chengkai Zhu,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 两个私有容量为零的量子信道（一个四能级信道与一个擦除概率为1/2的量子比特擦除信道）联合使用时，可实现每乘积次使用超过0.0001903私有比特的私密通信，从而解决了量子信息论中长期存在的私有容量超激活问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有噪量子信道上进行私密通信，既要求向接收方可靠传输，又要求对环境保密。一个长期未解的核心问题是：两个私有容量均为零的信道，能否联合实现私密通信。该问题关系到私有容量这一指标是否足以刻画信道的保密通信价值。

Method: 作者构造了一个四能级信道和一个擦除概率为1/2的量子比特擦除信道，二者各自的私有容量均为零。通过联合编码方案，使接收方获得线性信息增益，同时环境泄漏至多为二次增长，并借助固定的联合测量与经典编码来实现私密性。初始激活实例通过与大语言模型交互发现，结果已在Lean 4中形式化验证。

Result: 两个零私有容量信道的联合使用实现了超过0.0001903私有比特/乘积次使用的私密通信速率。这种超激活现象在独立的经典无记忆窃听信道中不可能出现。

Conclusion: 研究表明，信道单独的私有容量并不能决定其在安全通信中的实际价值；私有容量为零并不意味着信道在联合使用时无用于私密通信。

Abstract: Private communication over a noisy quantum channel requires reliable transmission to the receiver and secrecy from the environment. Whether two channels with zero private capacity can jointly enable private communication is a longstanding open problem in quantum information theory. Here we resolve this problem by exhibiting a four-level channel and a qubit erasure channel with half erasure probability, each with zero private capacity, whose joint use achieves more than 0.0001903 private bits per product use. The encoding gives the receiver a linear information gain with at most quadratic environmental leakage, enabling privacy through a fixed joint measurement and classical coding. This superactivation, impossible for independent classical memoryless wiretap channels, shows that a channel's private capacity alone does not determine its value for secure communication. The initial activation example was identified through interactions with large language models, and the result has been formalized in Lean 4.

</details>


### [103] [Noether Symmetries Generate Deterministic Energy-Harvesting Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10533)
*Ali Akil,M. Hamed Mohammady,Zihan Wang,Oscar Dahlsten*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究确定性能量收集（DEH）何时可能，提出利用源-收集器动力学的连续对称性与守恒的Noether荷来构造大量同时具备DEH能力的源态，并给出不对称性界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEH指从涨落源吸收能量而不吸收熵，已有工作仅给出零散实例，缺乏识别DEH可行源态的一般性原理。作者希望找到超越已有例子的通用构造路线。

Method: 论证连续对称性对应守恒Noether荷，在收集器边界态对称不变的条件下，诱导出源侧轨道，轨道上所有态实现同一确定性收集跃迁；将Noether定理推广到广义概率理论以推广主结果；并以Jaynes-Cummings模型、三自旋XX链和SU(2)模型作示例。

Result: 只要存在一个DEH可行源态且其电荷扇区之间具有非零不对称性，就能产生无穷多个同时DEH可行的源态；建立了收集的不对称性上界，表明源不对称性平均不会增加，而DEH恰好饱和该界并完全保持不对称性。

Conclusion: 对称性结构为识别DEH可行源态提供了通用途径，并给出不对称性上界，凸显不对称性在确定性能量收集中的核心作用。

Abstract: We consider the general principles for when deterministic energy harvesting (DEH) is possible. DEH means absorbing energy from a fluctuating source without entropy being absorbed. We show that the symmetry structure of the source--harvester dynamics gives a general route beyond existing examples to identify DEH capable source states. Any continuous symmetry with a conserved Noether charge induces a source-side orbit of states that all implement the same deterministic harvester transition, provided the harvester boundary states are symmetry invariant. Consequently, one DEH capable source state with nonzero asymmetry between charge sectors can generate infinitely many simultaneously DEH capable source states. A Jaynes-Cummings model, a three-spin XX chain, and an SU(2) model illustrate the construction. We further extend Noether's theorem to generalised probabilistic theories and thereby generalise our main result. We further establish an asymmetry bound for harvesting, showing that source asymmetry cannot increase on average, while DEH saturates the bound exactly preserving asymmetry.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Distinguishability Transitions from Global Quantum Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09296)
*Catherine McCarthy,Sarang Gopalakrishnan,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究贝叶斯分类器利用少量测量结果区分多个随机量子态的能力，发现区分能力在变量 x=m/ln k 控制下发生相变，并给出阈值 x_c。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用时间演化生成的量子态在局部测量下表现为随机且无特征的输出，对所有态都相同；但计算基下的全局快照却能有效区分量子态。作者希望探究在少量测量次数下，分类器能否以及何时能够区分大量随机量子态。

Method: 采用贝叶斯分类器对多个随机量子态进行判别，使用少量测量结果；定义控制变量 x = m / ln k（m 为测量次数/shots，k 为候选态数量），分析分类器的区分能力；并将结果推广到接近反集中（anti-concentration）起始阶段的低深度电路，以及含噪声电路和相位随机态。

Result: 识别出分类器区分 k 个候选态（m 次测量）能力的一个相变，该相变由变量 x = m / ln k 控制，并推导出阈值 x_c。结果可推广至接近反集中起始的低深度电路、含噪电路以及相位随机态。

Conclusion: 少量全局测量即可有效区分随机量子态，其可区分性存在由 m/ln k 控制的相变阈值 x_c，且该现象在低深度、含噪及相位随机等情形下依然成立。

Abstract: Quantum states generated by generic time evolution are locally featureless: local measurements return random outcomes that are identical for all states. However, global snapshots in the computational basis are surprisingly effective at distinguishing between quantum states. We explore the ability of Bayesian classifiers to discriminate between many random quantum states using a small number of measurement outcomes. We identify a phase transition in the ability of the classifier to distinguish between $k$ candidate states with $m$ shots controlled by the variable $x = m / \ln k$, and derive the threshold value $x_c$. We extend our results to low-depth circuits near the onset of anti-concentration, as well as to noisy circuits and phase-random states.

</details>


### [105] [Transient and universal regimes in quantum reaction-transport kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09305)
*Hossein Hosseinabadi,Roderich Moessner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 量子二元湮灭 A+A→∅ 在上临界维度 D_c=2 以下出现奇异涨落修正，低维下涨落主导渐近动力学：D<2 进入输运限制区，D=1 时为 n~t^{-1/2} 且受量子-Zeno 尺度控制，D=2 出现对数修正，D>2 恢复平均场标度；涨落还诱导有效弹性碰撞，可在 D>1 形成准稳态热化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上量子反应-输运系统的弛豫按反应与输运的相对时间尺度被划分为反应限制或输运限制。作者希望检验这一预期在低维下是否仍然成立，并厘清涨落对渐近动力学的定量影响。

Method: 以量子二元湮灭 A+A→∅ 为模型，通过分析涨落修正与上临界维度 D_c=2，考察不同维度下设失（反应）与相干输运的竞争；同时分析涨落诱导的有效弹性碰撞及其对动量分布的重新分配。

Result: 反应过程在 D_c=2 以下获得奇异涨落修正并主导渐近动力学。D<2 时涨落主导；一维下平均场弛豫变为瞬态，系统被驱动到输运限制区，n~t^{-1/2}，即使极弱损耗也由量子-Zeno 尺度控制。D=2 出现对数修正；D>2 渐近恢复平均场标度。在 D_c 及以下，平均场行为仍可在参数化长的跨越时间内保持。此外，涨落生成有效弹性碰撞；在 D>1 中其重新分配动量布居，且在输运限制区比设失更快，使系统趋向准稳态热态。

Conclusion: 低维量子反应-输运系统不遵循简单的反应限制/输运限制二分法：涨落主导其渐近动力学并改变标度律。上临界维度及以下存在长跨越时间，使平均场行为在有限尺度上仍可观测；涨落诱导的有效弹性碰撞可在无微观相干相互作用时驱动准稳态热化。

Abstract: Quantum reaction-transport systems consist of coherently propagating particles that irreversibly react upon encounter. Their relaxation is commonly classified as reaction-limited or transport-limited, depending on the relative timescales of reaction and particle transport. We show that this expectation fails in low dimensions by studying the quantum binary annihilation, $A+A \to \emptyset$, where reaction processes acquire singular fluctuation corrections below the upper critical dimension $D_c=2$. Consequently, fluctuations dominate the asymptotic kinetics for $D<2$. In one dimension, they render mean-field relaxation transient and drive the system toward a transport-limited regime with $n\sim t^{-1/2}$, governed by a quantum-Zeno scale even for arbitrarily weak loss. At $D=2$, the kinetics acquires logarithmic corrections, whereas above two dimensions mean-field scaling is asymptotically restored. At and below the upper critical dimension, mean-field behavior can nevertheless persist over parametrically long crossover times before the asymptotic fluctuation-dominated regime emerges. We also show that the same fluctuations generate effective elastic collisions despite the absence of microscopic coherent interactions. In $D>1$, these collisions redistribute momentum populations and are parametrically faster than losses in the transport-limited regime, allowing the system to approach a quasi-stationary thermal state.

</details>


### [106] [Exact asymptotic equivalence between precisions of record-times and integrated currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09479)
*Alberto Garilli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明连续时间马尔可夫跳过程中积分电流涨落与电流纪录事件时间统计量严格等价：积分电流的Fano因子等于纪录间隔时间（inter-record waiting times）的变异系数平方，并给出基于Narayana数的生成函数精确表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于电流涨落与纪录时间关系的结果仅适用于单环（unicyclic）网络，缺乏对一般网络的统一刻画。作者希望揭示电流精度的结构来源，并将其推广到任意有限状态图。

Method: 在纯概率框架下，利用纪录事件将系统重置到固定状态这一性质，证明纪录间隔时间构成更新结构（renewal structure）；借助由Narayana数支配的组合组织，精确表达纪录间隔时间的生成函数，从而推导Fano因子与变异系数平方的等式。

Result: 在极小假设下，积分电流的Fano因子精确等于纪录间隔等待时间的变异系数平方；该等价关系将单环网络的结果推广至任意有限状态图，并给出以纪录时间可观测量表示电流涨落的统一形式。

Conclusion: 电流精度可由类首达事件（first-passage-like）统计量刻画，纪录时间统计为电流涨落提供了统一的结构性解释。

Abstract: We establish an exact equivalence between the fluctuations of integrated currents and the temporal statistics of current record events in continuous-time Markov jump processes. Specifically, we show that, under minimal assumptions, the Fano factor of an integrated current coincides exactly with the squared coefficient of variation of the inter-record waiting times. This result is obtained by showing that inter-record times induce a renewal structure, since each record event resets the system to a fixed state, and that their generating function can be expressed exactly in terms of a combinatorial organization governed by Narayana numbers. The equivalence extends previous results obtained for unicyclic networks to arbitrary finite-state graphs and provides a unified representation of current fluctuations in terms of record-time observables. The results are derived within a purely probabilistic framework and clarify the structural origin of current precision in terms of first-passage-like event statistics.

</details>


### [107] [Gap-controlled thermalization in a SSH model version of the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09498)
*José A. Aké,Gerardo G. Naumis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究二聚化FPUT链（经典SSH模型类比）中声学-光学能隙如何调控非线性热化，发现当二聚化强度达到最大值一半时出现锐利的隔离阈值，由首次umklapp过程决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典非谐晶格中，带隙会重塑驱动非线性模式混合的共振结构，但光谱带隙究竟阻碍还是促进长时间热化尚不明确，因此需要定量研究能隙对弛豫过程的控制作用。

Method: 构建具有交替弹簧常数的二聚化FPUT链作为经典SSH模型类比，在α型（三次）非线性下，通过长时间辛几何模拟追踪模式能量与谱熵，分析声学-光学能隙对弛豫的影响。

Result: 当二聚化强度达到其最大值的一半时出现锐利的隔离阈值，由首次umklapp过程触发，即两个区边界声学声子融合为一个区中心光学声子；阈值以下，三波声学-声学-光学散射在约1e4个振荡周期的时间尺度上激活光学支，阈值以上则两支保持动力学隔离。边界条件还会改变这一图像，对特定模式激发产生长寿命粘滞态。

Conclusion: 声子带隙可作为非线性能量输运的可调、动量选择性滤波器，为控制二聚化或拓扑带隙非线性晶格中的热化提供了通用途径。

Abstract: In classical anharmonic lattices, the resonance structure driving nonlinear mode mixing is reshaped by band gaps. However, it remains unclear whether a spectral gap hinders or promotes long-time thermalization. Here we study a dimerized Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou chain - a classical SSH model analogue with alternating spring constants - and quantify how the acoustic-optical gap controls relaxation under alpha-type (cubic) nonlinearity. Tracking modal energies and spectral entropy via long-time symplectic simulations, we find a sharp isolation threshold when the dimerization strength reaches half its maximum value, set by the onset of the first umklapp process that allows two zone-boundary acoustic phonons to fuse into a zone-center optical phonon; below this threshold, three-wave acoustic-acoustic-optical scattering activates the optical branch on timescales of order 1e4 oscillation periods, while above it the bands remain dynamically isolated. We further show that boundary conditions reshape this picture, producing long-lived sticky states for specific mode excitations. These results establish the phononic gap as a tunable, momentum-selective filter for nonlinear energy transport, suggesting a general route to controlling thermalization in dimerized or topologically gapped nonlinear lattices.

</details>


### [108] [Topological instability and reentrant crystallization in active solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09500)
*Zory Davoyan,Aditya Jha,Anton Souslov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将KTHNY缺陷介导熔化理论推广至具有非互易（奇）弹性与Cosserat（微极性）耦合的活性固体，发现存在一个非互易阈值，超过该阈值时二维熔化由"拓扑不稳定性"机制主导，缺陷对在任何温度下增殖，表现为零温转变；在更高活性下还出现重入结晶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典KTHNY理论描述二维平衡固体的缺陷解绑熔化，但活性固体兼具非互易弹性和微极性耦合，其熔化行为是否仍遵循传统图像尚不清楚。作者希望厘清活性如何改变二维熔化路径，并寻找可能的普适新机制。

Method: 采用场论分析与数值模拟相结合的方法，在KTHNY框架内引入微扰的非互易弹性和Cosserat耦合；通过缺陷对逸度刻画熔化转变，并考察不同活性强度下的相行为。

Result: 在微扰小非互易下熔化仍由缺陷解绑驱动，但转变温度因活性而移动；当非互易达到阈值时出现定性不同的"拓扑不稳定性"熔化，缺陷对在所有温度下增殖，形成零温转变；更高活性下观察到重入结晶，即使温度超过平衡晶体熔点，准长程序仍可存续。

Conclusion: 核心能量驱动的缺陷增殖可作为二维熔化的一条普适且尚未被充分探索的新路径，活性固体只是该拓扑不稳定性的一个实例。

Abstract: We extend the KTHNY theory of defect-mediated melting in two dimensions to the case of active solids exhibiting both non-reciprocal (odd) elasticity and Cosserat (micropolar) coupling. For perturbatively small values of non-reciprocity, melting still proceeds via defect unbinding, but the melting temperature shifts due to activity. However, at a threshold value of non-reciprocity, we discover a qualitatively distinct mechanism for 2D melting, which we call topological instability. This melting occurs through the proliferation of defect pairs at any temperature. We use a combination of field theory and simulations to characterize this zero-temperature transition in terms of the defect-pair fugacity. Surprisingly, for higher activity values, we find reentrant crystallization, where the quasi-long-range order survives even at temperatures for which the equilibrium crystal would melt. Although active solids present one realization of this topological instability, we envision core-energy-driven defect proliferation as a generic and unexplored route for two-dimensional melting.

</details>


### [109] [The Role of Concentration in Determining NaCl Nucleation Mechanism: A Story of Pathways Coexistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09518)
*Porhouy Minh,Sapna Sarupria*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文用∞RETIS无偏路径采样研究NaCl在不同过饱和浓度下的成核路径，发现所有浓度遵循同一反应坐标，但非晶聚集与结晶的解耦程度随过饱和度连续变化，路径共存于单一反应通道中，从而调和了既有争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水溶液中NaCl成核是否涉及非晶离子聚集、以及两步机制在何种过饱和区间出现，学界长期存在争论。作者希望在不预设机理假设的前提下识别真实成核路径，以澄清矛盾报道。

Method: 在12、13、14 mol/kg（S=3.2、3.5、3.8）三个浓度下，采用∞RETIS无偏路径采样模拟NaCl成核；结合反应坐标分析、联合与条件概率分布、扩散张量分析及条件自由能图景，无先验机理假设地辨识反应路径。

Result: 三个浓度受同一反应坐标支配，说明核生长与结构有序化是跨越势垒的关键；但反应轨迹性质随过饱和度改变：低浓度下成核以团簇尺寸增大与有序化高度耦合的方式进行，接近一步机制；浓度升高时非晶聚集与结晶逐渐解耦，先发生显著非晶生长再结晶的路径概率增大。这些路径共存于单一宽反应通道，而非经由不同的亚稳中间态。

Conclusion: 结果调和了关于NaCl成核机制的既有矛盾报道，支持将成核视为一组相互竞争、相对概率随过饱和度连续变化的反应路径集合；并表明在经典成核框架内即可涌现显著机理多样性，无需引入截然不同的非经典描述。

Abstract: Deciphering the microscopic details of crystal nucleation remains a key open problem in chemical physics. For aqueous sodium chloride (NaCl), the extent to which nucleation involves amorphous ion aggregation, and the supersaturation regime in which two-step-like behavior emerges, remains actively debated. In this work, we examine NaCl nucleation at 12, 13, and 14 mol/kg ($S = 3.2, 3.5, 3.8$) using unbiased path sampling with $\infty$RETIS. Through a combination of reaction coordinate analysis, joint and conditional probability distributions, diffusion tensor analysis, and conditional free energy landscapes, we identify nucleation pathways without a priori mechanistic assumptions. While all concentrations are governed by the same reaction coordinate, indicating that nucleus growth and structural ordering are central to crossing the barrier, the nature of reactive trajectories changes with supersaturation. At lower concentrations, nucleation mainly occurs via tightly coupled increases in cluster size and ordering, compatible with a one-step-like mechanism. As concentration increases, amorphous aggregation becomes increasingly decoupled from crystallization, and pathways involving substantial amorphous growth prior to crystallization become progressively more probable. Importantly, these pathways coexist within a single broad reaction channel rather than proceeding through distinct metastable intermediate states. Together, these results reconcile earlier, contradictory reports on NaCl nucleation mechanisms and support a view of nucleation as an ensemble of competing reactive pathways whose relative probabilities vary continuously with supersaturation. More broadly, our findings illustrate that substantial mechanistic diversity can emerge within a classical nucleation framework, without requiring distinct non-classical descriptions.

</details>


### [110] [Energy Transport Structure and Fluctuation Theorem in Nonreciprocal Harmonic Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09593)
*Ziwei Cheng,Geng Li,Zhanchun Tu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对一维非对称耦合谐振子链，建立了非互易热输运的精确输运结构，揭示热流方向由温度偏置与有效方向不对称性的竞争决定，并给出能量平衡与涨落定理的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易相互作用打破了前向与后向响应的对称性，从而根本性地改变能量输运，但缺乏对其精确输运结构和能量-涨落层面的统一描述。

Method: 采用格林函数方法，研究具有非对称最近邻耦合的一维谐振子链，推导稳态能量平衡关系，并在涨落层面分析两热库热交换的随机流及其联合标度累积生成函数。

Result: 热流方向不仅由温度偏置决定，还取决于其与有效方向不对称性的竞争，该竞争可使热流反向，实现冷到热的输运；非互易性引入与相互作用反对称部分相关的额外功率通道，导致包含两热库热流和一个非互易功率流的广义稳态能量平衡；与两热库的热交换构成关联但不同的随机流，其联合标度累积生成函数满足精确的Gallavotti-Cohen对称性。

Conclusion: 研究建立了非互易热输运的统一能量学与涨落框架，表明方向性相互作用可作为控制非平衡能量流的主动资源。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal interactions fundamentally alter energy transport by breaking the symmetry between forward and backward responses. Here, we uncover an exact transport structure for a one-dimensional harmonic chain with asymmetric nearest-neighbor couplings. Using a Green-function approach, we demonstrate that the direction of heat transport is determined not solely by the temperature bias, but by its competition with an effective directional asymmetry. This competition can reverse the direction of heat flow, enabling cold-to-hot transport. We further show that nonreciprocity introduces an additional power channel associated with the antisymmetric sector of the interaction, leading to a generalized steady-state energy balance involving two reservoir heat currents and a nonreciprocal power current. At the fluctuation level, the heat exchanges with the two reservoirs constitute correlated yet distinct stochastic currents, whose joint scaled cumulant generating function obeys an exact Gallavotti-Cohen symmetry. Together, these results establish a unified energetic and fluctuation framework for nonreciprocal heat transport and demonstrate that directional interactions can serve as an active resource for controlling nonequilibrium energy flows.

</details>


### [111] [Renewal process's guide to fractional Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09620)
*Hong Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用具有任意分布等待时间的碰撞更新过程推导Boltzmann方程，进而证明指数等待时间对应经典Navier-Stokes方程、幂律等待时间对应分数阶Navier-Stokes方程，从而给出求解这两类方程的随机方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Navier-Stokes方程尚未解决，是流体力学中的核心难题，也是千禧年难题之一。Hilbert在1900年提出通过建立微观动力学与宏观连续方程之间联系来攻克该问题的思路，其关键桥梁是Boltzmann方程的推导与概率理论。本文旨在沿此思路，借助随机过程建立微观与宏观的对应关系，为求解经典及分数阶Navier-Stokes方程提供新途径。

Method: 采用具有任意分布等待时间的碰撞更新过程（collision renewal process），推导描述粒子速度与位移概率演化的Boltzmann方程；在此基础上分别讨论指数分布等待时间与幂律等待时间两种情形，证明其分别等价于经典Navier-Stokes方程与分数阶Navier-Stokes方程。由于该更新过程是随机过程，可通过轨迹的随机模拟获得相应解。

Result: 证明了两点等价关系：等待时间服从指数分布的碰撞更新过程等价于经典Navier-Stokes方程；等待时间服从幂律分布的碰撞更新过程等价于分数阶Navier-Stokes方程。由此，随机模拟轨迹即可求解对应的经典和分数阶Navier-Stokes方程。

Conclusion: 本文建立了从微观随机碰撞过程到宏观（分数阶）Navier-Stokes方程的推导桥梁，提出了求解经典与分数阶Navier-Stokes方程的随机（蒙特卡洛式轨迹模拟）方法，为Hilbert所设想的微观-宏观联系路径提供了具体的概率论实现。

Abstract: The Navier-Stokes equations, which remain unsolved, are crucial equations in fluid mechanics. Discovering the solutions to the Navier-Stokes equations is one of the challenging Millennium problems. In 1900, Hilbert proposed a potential approach to tackle this problem by establishing the relationship between microscopic dynamics and the macroscopic continuum equations. The key bridge is the derivation of Boltzmann equation and the theory of probability. In this paper, we shall use the collision renewal process with arbitrarily distributed waiting times to derive the Boltzmann equation for the time evolution of the probability of the velocity and the displacement of the particle, based on which we prove that the renewal process with exponential collision waiting time is equivalent to the classical Navier-Stokes equations, and that with power-law waiting time is equivalent to the fractional Navier-Stokes equations. Since the collision renewal process with arbitrarily distributed waiting times is a random process and is easy to perform the stochastic simulations of trajectories to obtain the corresponding solution, we actually find a stochastic approach to solve the classical and fractional Navier-Stokes equations.

</details>


### [112] [Intermittent continuous-time random walks under renewal reset mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09738)
*Guohua Li,Hong Zhang,Yuexiong Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了随机重置下的间歇性连续时间随机游走（CTRW），以跳跃与重置等待时间的较小者作为更新时刻，二者可服从任意分布。研究推导了更新重置与马尔可夫重置下的控制方程和Montroll-Weiss方程，证明了非平衡稳态的存在性，考察了MSD的单调性与渐近稳定性，并计算首次到达时间以量化搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机重置作为复杂无序环境中实用高效的搜索策略长期受到关注。文章基于跳跃与重置之间的竞争机制，旨在刻画二者共同决定更新过程的间歇性CTRW，以更真实地描述搜索行为。

Method: 以跳跃和重置中的较小等待时间作为更新时刻，且每次更新后重新独立抽取二者的等待时间。推导更新重置与马尔可夫重置下的控制方程与Montroll-Weiss方程；证明指数与幂律等待时间下更新重置机制存在非平衡稳态；对指数和高斯跳跃步长计算均方位移（MSD）的单调性与渐近稳定性；计算首次到达时间以量化搜索效率。

Result: 证明了更新重置机制在跳跃与重置等待时间服从任意指数和幂律分布时存在非平衡稳态；对指数与高斯跳跃步长分析了MSD的单调性与渐近稳定性；对于指数跳跃与指数重置WTD、幂律跳跃与指数重置WTD、指数跳跃与幂律重置WTD，模型给出到达任意固定位置的有限平均首次到达时间（MFAT）；而幂律跳跃与幂律重置WTD下MFAT发散。

Conclusion: 基于竞争机制的间歇性CTRW模型可用于多种物理情景，例如动物觅食失败后返回巢穴的觅食策略，或智能机器人在长时间运行后返回能量补充点的工作规划。

Abstract: Stochastic resetting as a practical and efficient search strategy in complex and disordered environments has long been a topic of interest to researchers. Based on the competition between jumping and resetting, this article proposes and investigates intermittent continuous-time random walks (CTRWs) under stochastic resetting, using the smaller waiting time for jump and reset as the renewal time, where the waiting times for both jump and reset can have arbitrary distributions. After each renewal event, the system will proceed with new waiting times for jump and reset regardless of their previous histories. We study the governing equation and Montroll-Weiss equation with renewal resetting, as well as the Markovian resetting for intermittent CTRWs. We prove the existence of non-equilibrium stationary states within the renewal reset mechanism when the jump and reset waiting times follow any exponential and power law distributions. For exponential and Gaussian distributed jump lengths, we examine the mean square displacements (MSDs) of particles to determine their monotonicity and asymptotic stability. Moreover, we calculate the first-arrival time to quantify search efficiency, and validate the intermittent CTRWs under renewal resetting lead to a finite mean first-arrival time (MFAT) to any fixed position for exponential jump and reset waiting time distributions (WTDs), power-law jump and exponential reset WTDs, as well as exponential jump and power-law reset WTDs. However, the MFAT diverges for power-law jump and reset WTDs. The intermittent CTRW model, which is based on the competition mechanism, can be applied to many physical scenarios, such as the foraging strategy of animals that return to their nests after an unsuccessful foraging attempt, or the work planning of intelligent robots that return to energy replenishment points after prolonged operation.

</details>


### [113] [Quantifying cell shape and density fluctuations in epithelial tissue in vivo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09874)
*Jake Turley,Helen Weavers,Paul Martin,Isaac V. Chenchiah,Tanniemola B. Liverpool*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个描述上皮细胞形状动力学的理论框架，将细胞建模为可变尺寸和长宽比的软椭球，经粗粒化得到连续随机微分方程并推导时空关联函数，用于拟合果蝇蛹翅发育数据并提取组织有效"刚度"等关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织通常具有细胞形状和大小的异质性，偏离平均形状尺寸的局部涨落可跨组织传播，可能反映组织有效"刚度"或刚性等特性。为刻画这些涨落的平均行为与波动规律，需要建立一个整合二者动力学特征的理论框架。

Method: 将细胞建模为尺寸与长宽比可变的相互作用软椭球；对模型进行粗粒化处理，得到一组连续随机微分方程；再由此推导出时空关联函数。

Result: 推导出的时空关联函数与果蝇蛹翅（Drosophila pupal wing）发育过程的实验数据相吻合；从关联函数中可确定反映活跃细胞形状变化及组织有效"刚度"的关键参数。

Conclusion: 该框架能够统一描述上皮细胞形状的平均行为与涨落模式，关联函数与实验一致并可用于提取组织力学的关键参数，为通过形状涨落推断组织有效刚度等特性提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Controlling changes in cell shape are crucial for many biological processes, such as tissue development and wound healing. Tissues typically are heterogeneous with a variety of cell shapes and sizes. Of particular interest are local deviations from an average cell shape and size. These fluctuations may extend and transmit across tissues, potentially offering valuable insights into tissue characteristics such as variations in effective "stiffness" or rigidity. In this study, we present a theoretical framework that captures the dynamics of epithelial cell shapes within tissue, incorporating both their average behaviour and fluctuation patterns. We model cells as interacting soft ellipsoids of varying size and aspect ratio. Coarse-graining our model, we obtain a set of continuum stochastic differential equations from which we derive spatial-temporal correlation functions. These correlation functions fit with our experimental data from the developmental process of the \textit{Drosophila} pupal wing. From the correlation functions, critical parameters representing active cell shape changes and effective tissue "stiffness" can be determined.

</details>


### [114] [Efficient protocol for the Markovian Mpemba effect in $N$-level systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09988)
*David Gelbwaser-Klimovsky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究马尔可夫Mpemba效应的产生条件，给出三能级系统的充要条件，并提出在N能级系统中寻找该效应参数的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mpemba效应是一种反直觉现象：初始偏离平衡更远的系统反而可能更快热化。但该效应罕见，难以找到产生它的参数，使实验设计和应用开发受限。

Method: 作者推导三能级系统中马尔可夫Mpemba效应的充要条件，阐明其物理机制；并基于对三能级情形机制的理解，设计一种高效算法来确定N能级系统中产生该效应所需的参数。

Result: 得到了三能级系统中马尔可夫Mpemba效应的充要条件，并成功将该理解推广为适用于N能级系统的高效参数搜索算法。

Conclusion: 该协议有望推动在大型系统中实现Mpemba效应实验及相关应用。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect is an anomalous phenomenon in which the time to thermalize does not depend monotonically on the distance from equilibrium, allowing systems with an initially larger temperature difference to thermalize before those with a smaller one. The rarity of the effect makes it hard to find parameters that produce it, complicating the design of experiments and the development of applications. Here, we find necessary and sufficient conditions for the Markovian Mpemba effect in three-level systems and explain its underlying physical mechanisms. Based on our understanding of the three-level case, we develop an efficient algorithm to determine the parameters for the effect in $N$-level systems. This protocol could open the door for the realization of Mpemba experiments and related applications in large systems.

</details>


### [115] [Infinite ergodic theory and functional statistics of non-confined Feller process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10106)
*Vicenç Méndez*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究非约束Feller过程（具不可归一化平稳密度与无限均值回返时间）的可加可观测量，重点考察局部时场的空间结构，证明长时极限下局部时可因子化为空间剖面（由不可归一化平稳密度支配）与单一Mittag-Leffler随机振幅（控制时间涨落）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该过程经确定性重标度后等价于平方Bessel过程，其平稳概率密度不可归一化、回返时间均值为无限，属于无限遍历理论范畴；作者希望从空间场视角统一理解此类状态依赖乘性噪声下的无限遍历现象。

Method: 分析非约束Feller过程的可加泛函与局部时场；利用确定性重标度将其与平方Bessel过程对应；在长时间极限下对局部时进行因子化分解，比较空间剖面与时间涨落；并分别处理有限区间占据时与非可积幂次可观测量的极限分布。

Result: 局部时在长时极限下可因子化：空间剖面由不可归一化平稳密度刻画，时间涨落由单一Mittag-Leffler随机振幅控制；局部时的归一化空间相关收敛到与两个观测水平无关的普适常数；有限区间占据时服从Darling-Kac涨落；非可积幂次可观测量则呈现自相似平方Bessel型极限而非Mittag-Leffler统计。

Conclusion: 该空间场视角为状态依赖乘性噪声下的无限遍历理论提供了统一框架，揭示了局部时空间结构与时间统计的分离机制及不同可观测量的两类极限行为。

Abstract: We study additive observables of a non-confined Feller process characterized by a non- normalizable stationary probability density and return times with infinite mean. The process is equivalent, after a deterministic rescaling, to a squared Bessel process, but we focus here on a question: the spatial structure of its local-time field. We show that the local time admits a fac- torization in the long time limit. Its spatial profile is governed by the non-normalizable stationary density, whereas its temporal fluctuations are controlled by a single Mittag-Leffler random ampli- tude. As a consequence, normalized spatial correlations of the local time converge to a universal constant independent of the two observation levels. Occupation times of finite intervals follow as corollaries and display Darling-Kac fluctuations. In contrast, non-integrable power observables exhibit self-similar squared-Bessel-type limits rather than Mittag-Leffler statistics. This spatial- field perspective provides a unifying framework for infinite ergodic theory under state-dependent multiplicative noise.

</details>


### [116] [Anizotropic Ising Model on 2D Kagomé Lattice as an Inhomogeneous XYZ Integrable Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10163)
*Shahane A. Khachatryan,Hrachya Babujian,Ara G. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究kagomé格子上双交替耦合的广义非均匀二维Ising模型，将其局域Boltzmann权重映射为满足自由费米子条件的非对称八顶点R-矩阵，分析两种耦合约束下的Yang–Baxter结构并导出相应一维量子自旋链，同时计算部分各向异性模型的自由能与比热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在推广kagomé格子上的Ising模型，引入三个方向上的两套交替耦合，以统一处理非均匀与各向异性情形，并借助可积性框架揭示其与一维量子自旋链及配分函数零点结构的联系。

Method: 将局域Boltzmann权重映射到满足自由费米子条件的非对称八顶点R-矩阵；对J'_i=J_i和J'_i=-J_i两种情形分析Yang–Baxter结构，并在各向异性极限下导出一维量子自旋链；对部分各向异性模型计算自由能与比热。

Result: 两个交替耦合情形均可嵌入自由费米子八顶点框架；J'_i=J_i对应横向场Ising型哈密顿量，J'_i=-J_i对应具有分级置换结构且配分函数零点发生移动的修正链；部分各向异性模型给出自由能与比热的解析结果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明广义非均匀kagomé Ising模型具有统一的自由费米子可积描述，两种耦合约束分别导向不同的量子自旋链及零点结构，为研究kagomé体系的可积性与临界行为提供了框架。

Abstract: We investigate a generalized inhomogeneous two-dimensional Ising model on the kagomé lattice with two alternating couplings $J_i$ and $J'_i$, $i=1,2,3$, along the three lattice directions. The local Boltzmann weights are mapped onto a non-symmetric eight-vertex $R$-matrix satisfying the free-fermion condition for arbitrary values of the six couplings. We analyze the Yang--Baxter structure for the cases $J'_i=J_i$ and $J'_i=-J_i$ and derive the corresponding one-dimensional quantum spin chains in the anisotropic limit. The first case yields a transverse-field Ising-type Hamiltonian, while the second leads to a modified chain with a graded permutation structure and shifted partition-function zeros. For a partially anisotropic model, we also obtain the free energy and specific heat.

</details>


### [117] [Italian Business-to-Business Invoicing Data: A Network Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10227)
*Valerio Astuti,Daniele Piras*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 基于意大利税务局电子发票全量数据，首次刻画了意大利企业对企业商业关系网络，揭示其无标度结构与重要性高度集中特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用独特的全量行政数据，填补对意大利生产系统企业间网络的实证刻画空白，并给出地理与行业分解。

Method: 基于意大利税务局采集的电子发票全库，构建企业对企业（B2B）商业关系网络；统计每家企业买方数与卖方数分布、企业中心性及企业间平均距离，并进行地理和行业分解；对各类分布估计尾部指数。

Result: 每家企业买方数与卖方数分布呈重尾，表明网络为无标度结构；中心性分布同样呈重尾，重要性高度集中于少数企业；所有分布的尾部指数中，下游（买方侧）指数均低于上游（卖方侧）。

Conclusion: 意大利B2B网络具有无标度特征且重要性高度集中，买方侧尾部更厚，反映下游环节的异质性更强。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive description of the network of Italian Business-to-Business commercial relationships, based on the universe of electronic invoices collected by the Italian Tax Office. The firm-to-firm detail of the data is exploited to describe the distribution of the numbers of buyers (customers) and sellers (suppliers) per firm, the centrality of each firm in the production network, and the average distance between firms. We characterize for the first time the firm-to-firm network describing the Italian production system, and present its geographic and sectoral breakdown. The analysis reveals heavy tailed distributions for the numbers of buyers and sellers per firm, implying a scale-free structure of the network. The distributions of centrality values display heavy tails as well, indicating a strong concentration of importance in a relatively small number of firms. We estimate the tail exponents for all these distributions, finding in all cases lower exponents for downstream (buyer-side) distributions than for the upstream (seller-side) ones.

</details>


### [118] [Quantifying translational and bond-orientational order metrics in hyperuniform and nonhyperuniform many-particle systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10359)
*Anirban Mukherjee,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文引入键向序度量τ_O，与平移序度量τ_T构成(τ_T,τ_O)二元描述，统一刻画多粒子体系的平移与键向有序程度，并在2D/3D硬球、RSA以及2D隐身超均匀基态中计算二者随堆积分数/隐身参数的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化多粒子体系中的有序/无序程度是物理学、材料科学与数学中的未解难题，需要将平移序与键向序置于统一的两点统计框架下进行比较。

Method: 定义平移序度量τ_T为总相关函数h(r)的L²范数平方；引入键向类比τ_O，由加权总相关函数h_f(r)结合局部取向权重定义。在2D和3D中对两种非超均匀球堆积模型（平衡硬粒子、非平衡RSA）计算两度量随堆积分数φ的变化；并研究2D无序隐身超均匀（SHU）基态（0<χ<1/2）。

Result: 非超均匀体系中键向序始终次主导，但在平衡流体分支上端其相对幅度增大（2D强于3D），在晶体分支上两者可比；相同φ下平衡流体与RSA给出不同的(τ_T,τ_O)轨迹，显示制备依赖性。SHU无序相中键向序次主导，但趋近无序-有序阈值时相对幅度增大。三种模型中，超出泊松参考区后τ_T与τ_O正相关，τ_O随τ_T单调增加。

Conclusion: (τ_T,τ_O)为平移与键向有序提供了统一的两点统计描述，可区分不同制备路径的结构差异，并揭示键向序在接近有序转变时的相对增强。

Abstract: Quantifying the degree of order/disorder in many-particle systems remains an outstanding problem in physics, materials science, and mathematics. To this end, we consider the translational order metric $τ_T$, defined as the squared $L^2$ norm of the total correlation function $h(\mathbf{r})$, and introduce its bond-orientational analogue $τ_O$, defined from the weighted total correlation function $h_{\mathbf{f}}(\mathbf{r})$ using local orientational weights (S. Torquato et al., Phys. Rev. X 16, 011042 (2026)). The pair $(τ_T,τ_O)$ places both forms of order on a common two-point statistical footing. We compute both metrics for two nonhyperuniform sphere-packing models in 2D and 3D as functions of packing fraction $φ$: 1) equilibrium hard particles and 2) nonequilibrium random sequential addition (RSA) packings. For the nonhyperuniform systems, bond-orientational order remains subdominant along the equilibrium-fluid and RSA configurations; however, its magnitude relative to the translational metric increases near the upper end of the equilibrium-fluid branches, much more strongly in 2D than in 3D, and the two metrics become comparable along the sampled crystal branches. At common packing fractions, equilibrium fluids and RSA packings trace distinct $(τ_T,τ_O)$ trajectories, revealing preparation-dependent differences in structural order. As a representative hyperuniform family, we study 2D disordered stealthy hyperuniform (SHU) ground states for $0<χ<1/2$, where $χ$ is the stealthiness parameter. Within the disordered SHU phase, bond-orientational order remains subdominant, but its magnitude relative to the translational metric increases toward the disorder-to-order threshold. In all three models, $τ_T$ and $τ_O$ are positively correlated beyond the Poisson-reference regime: $τ_O$ increases monotonically with $τ_T$ across the sampled state points.

</details>


### [119] [The Arithmetic of Spectra: Factorization, Statistics, and Symmetric Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10391)
*A. Chaudhary*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究非相互作用量子系统单粒子谱的因子化，及其对多粒子统计的影响，并给出正则Bose/Fermi配分函数的统一组合框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单粒子谱的结构与多粒子统计之间存在联系，但缺乏一个统一的组合视角来系统处理谱的因子化问题。

Method: 用"谱字母表"表示单粒子配分函数，将张量因子化转化为字母表的乘法因子化，再借助对称函数与λ-环恒等式提升为正则Bose和Fermi配分函数。分析乘积谱如何源自不同因子化、反对称化如何分配到奇数个因子上，以及哪些张量因子化与固定谱相容。

Result: 建立了谱因子化与多粒子统计之间的联系，刻画了乘积谱的来源、反对称化在奇数个因子上的分配方式，以及固定谱下相容的张量因子化条件。

Conclusion: 谱因子化可通过组合方法系统研究，对称函数与λ-环为Bose和Fermi正则配分函数提供了合并的统一组合框架。

Abstract: We study factorizations of the single-particle spectrum of non-interacting quantum systems and their consequences for many-particle statistics. Representing the single-particle partition function by a spectral alphabet, tensor factorizations become multiplicative factorizations of the alphabet, which can be lifted to canonical Bose and Fermi partition functions using standard symmetric-function and $λ$-ring identities. We show how product spectra arise from different factorizations, how antisymmetrization can be assigned across an odd number of factors, and which tensor factorizations are compatible with a fixed spectrum. The paper therefore studies spectral factorization and provides a consolidated combinatorial framework for canonical Bose and Fermi partition functions.

</details>


### [120] [Protected domains and the cost of cooperation on weighted networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10463)
*Abhishek Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限接触结构下合作行为的演化：捐赠博弈中合作何时比背叛更易占据群体，取决于受保护域的形成与竞争，并给出了路径/环上的弱选择阈值、接触异质性与吸收时间等代价的精确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释一种有成本的遗传性状（合作）如何在有限接触结构中扩散：当后代能创造有利的局部环境时，集体竞争者可以维持。作者希望确定哪些有限接触结构能支撑这种集体竞争，并量化其优势所需付出的代价（接触异质性、精度、观测时间）。

Method: 个体进行捐赠博弈（收益b、成本c）；倒数加权接触决定平均收益与复制对象，源节点按全局收益选择。以"均匀引入的合作者比背叛者在互补实验中更常占据"作为合作被favored的判据。采用精确涨落响应与快速构型消除方法，识别受保护域的形成与竞争为控制过程；并用接触比之间的多项式关系分析无约束优化与反射对称性。

Result: 弱选择阈值下确界：四环b/c=3，五点路径b/c=7，六个及以上固定路径b/c=1。六点路径上全局界迫使近势垒设计落入该域层级。达到阈值1+ε需最小接触对比度656ε^{-2}[1+O(ε)]，中性吸收时间为ε^{-1}量级；确定了二者的权衡与设计容差，有限选择消耗相同余量。在小选择窗口内，当对比度超出精确最小值ε^{-1}量级时，最大固定优势为ε^{5/2}量级。

Conclusion: 改善入侵阈值往往需要更大接触异质性、更高精度与更长观测时间。有限接触结构的合作持续性由受保护域机制主导，阈值、对比度与吸收时间之间存在精确的权衡关系。

Abstract: A costly inherited trait can spread when its descendants create a favorable local environment. We ask which finite contact structures sustain such a collective competitor and what their advantage costs. Individuals play a donation game, conferring benefit $b$ at cost $c$. Reciprocal weighted contacts determine averaged payoffs and copying recipients, while sources are chosen globally according to payoff. Cooperation is favored when a uniformly introduced cooperator takes over more often than a defector in the complementary experiment. Exact fluctuation response and elimination of fast configurations identify the formation and competition of protected domains as the controlling processes. The sharp weak-selection threshold infima are $b/c=3$ on the four-cycle, $7$ on the five-site path, and $1$ on every fixed path with at least six sites. On the six-site path, a global bound forces near-barrier designs into this domain hierarchy. Polynomial relations among the contact ratios then control unrestricted optimization and imply eventual exact reflection symmetry. Reaching threshold $1+ε$ requires minimum contact contrast $656ε^{-2}[1+O(ε)]$ and neutral absorption time of order $ε^{-1}$. We determine their tradeoff and the associated design tolerance. Finite selection consumes the same margin. Within the controlled small-selection window, the maximal fixation advantage is of order $ε^{5/2}$ when the contrast exceeds its exact minimum by order $ε^{-1}$. Thus an improving invasion threshold can require increasing contact heterogeneity, precision and observation time.

</details>


### [121] [Optimal Intermediate Hamiltonians for Non-Equilibrium Free Energy Calculations: A Numerical Study of Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10519)
*David Beyer,Helmut Grubmüller*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过倾斜主方程表示MSE并结合自动微分，优化连接初末态的中间哈密顿量序列，从而提升Jarzynski估计的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jarzynski关系可从非平衡有限时间切换模拟估计平衡自由能差，但估计通常收敛很差，因为指数工作平均被稀有轨迹主导。作者希望找到使Jarzynski估计量均方误差(MSE)最小的中间哈密顿量序列以增强收敛。

Method: 针对离散时间马尔可夫模型，推导大样本极限下MSE的精确倾斜主方程(tilted-master-equation)表示，结合自动微分，对所有中间能量进行高效的基于梯度的最小化。并在三个复杂度递增的模型系统上应用：两态模型、双势阱势和位移势阱。

Result: 在三个系统中，最优中间哈密顿量在初始和最终时刻发生跳变。大量蒙特卡洛模拟显示，与线性及对数插值相比，最优中间态可将MSE降低一个数量级以上，在能量景观变化大时尤为显著。值得注意的是，它们并不一定比误差更大的中间态耗散更少的工作。

Conclusion: 研究结果为现实分子系统更高效的非平衡自由能计算提供启发式规则：最优中间哈密顿量在初末时刻跳变；对跨垒问题，势垒应快速降低后再升高；最小化耗散并不能保证更快收敛。

Abstract: The Jarzynski relation enables the estimation of equilibrium free energy differences from non-equilibrium, finite-time switching simulations. These estimates usually converge poorly because rare trajectories dominate the exponential work average. Here, we numerically determined and explored the sequence of intermediate Hamiltonians connecting initial and final states that minimize the mean squared error (MSE) of the Jarzynski estimator and thereby enhance convergence. For discrete-time Markov models, an exact tilted-master-equation representation of the MSE in the large-sample limit, combined with automatic differentiation, enables efficient gradient-based minimization over all intermediate energies. We applied our approach to three model systems of increasing complexity: a two-state model, a double-well potential, and a shifted potential well. In all three systems, the optimal intermediate Hamiltonians jump at the initial and final times. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations show that optimal intermediates can reduce the MSE by more than an order of magnitude compared with linear and logarithmic interpolation, most strongly for large changes in the energy landscape. Remarkably, they need not dissipate less work than intermediates yielding larger errors. Our results suggest heuristics for more efficient non-equilibrium free energy calculations of realistic molecular systems: optimal intermediate Hamiltonians jump at the initial and final times; for barrier-crossing problems, the barrier should be lowered rapidly and raised again later; and minimizing dissipation does not guarantee faster convergence.

</details>

### [122] [Curvature-Induced Geometric Universality in Non-Hermitian Anderson Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09562)
*Chen Wang,Run-Qiu Yang,X. R. Wang,Hechen Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出非厄米安德森相变存在一类由双曲几何（负曲率）控制的普适类，数值标度给出ν≃1，并用粗粒化Landau-Ginzburg分析解释其类平均场行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧氏空间中安德森相变的普适类主要由对称性与空间维度决定；作者希望探究空间曲率（双曲几何）是否可作为普适类分类的额外组织原则。

Method: 在类双曲格子上研究非厄米安德森相变；对多个不同{p,q}镶嵌结构进行参与比的有限尺寸标度分析，并辅以唯象粗粒化Landau-Ginzburg分析。

Result: 不同{p,q}镶嵌的有限尺寸标度均可用单参数标度坍缩描述，数值精度内临界指数一致为ν≃1；粗粒化分析表明指数型关联体积增长抑制临界涨落，从而解释了类平均场标度行为。

Conclusion: 空间曲率可作为超越传统维度-对称性分类的安德森相变普适类组织原则，几何控制的普适类在双曲格子上得到证据支持。

Abstract: In Euclidean space, universality classes of Anderson transitions are primarily determined by symmetry and spatial dimensionality. Here, we present evidence for a geometry-controlled universality class of non-Hermitian Anderson transitions on hyperbolic-like lattices. In this setting, critical behavior is influenced by the large-scale hyperbolic geometry, characterized by negative curvature, exponential volume growth, and a non-Euclidean notion of spatial scaling. Finite-size scaling of participation ratios across several distinct \( \{p,q\} \) tilings reveals one-parameter scaling collapses with a common critical exponent \( ν\simeq1 \) within numerical accuracy. A complementary phenomenological coarse-grained Landau-Ginzburg analysis shows how exponential correlation-volume growth suppresses critical fluctuations, offering a rationale for the observed mean-field-like scaling. Our results suggest that spatial curvature can act as an additional organizing principle for Anderson-transition universality beyond the conventional dimensionality- and symmetry-based classification.

</details>
